============================================================
SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
============================================================

This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.

The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.

     1	import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
     2	import { EB_Garamond } from "next/font/google";
     3	import { ScreenshotPreviewLayer } from "./ScreenshotPreviewLayer";
     4	import "./globals.css";
     5	
     6	const ebGaramond = EB_Garamond({
     7	  subsets: ["latin"],
     8	  style: "italic",
     9	  variable: "--font-eb-garamond",
    10	  weight: "500",
    11	});
    12	
    13	export const metadata: Metadata = {
    14	  metadataBase: new URL(
    15	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || "https://prahl.dev",
    16	  ),
    17	  title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    18	  description:
    19	    "Portfolio for Prahl.dev, a React Native full stack mobile developer building polished mobile apps, secure checkout flows, and production-ready Android pipelines.",
    20	  openGraph: {
    21	    title: "Prahl.dev | React Native Full Stack Mobile Developer",
    22	    description:
    23	      "React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, and Kotlin mobile portfolio.",
    24	    images: ["/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png"],
    25	  },
    26	};
    27	
    28	export const viewport: Viewport = {
    29	  width: "device-width",
    30	  initialScale: 1,
    31	};
    32	
    33	export default function RootLayout({
    34	  children,
    35	}: Readonly<{
    36	  children: React.ReactNode;
    37	}>) {
    38	  return (
    39	    <html lang="en">
    40	      <body className={ebGaramond.variable}>
    41	        {children}
    42	        <ScreenshotPreviewLayer />
    43	      </body>
    44	    </html>
    45	  );
    46	}

    61	
    62	function StackTerm({
    63	  icon,
    64	  imageAlt,
    65	  imageSrc,
    66	  iconStroke,
    67	  label,
    68	  postmark = false,
    69	  symbol: Symbol,
    70	  symbolLabel,
    71	}: {
    72	  icon?: SimpleIcon;
    73	  imageAlt?: string;
    74	  imageSrc?: string;
    75	  iconStroke?: string;
    76	  label: string;
    77	  postmark?: boolean;
    78	  symbol?: LucideIcon;
    79	  symbolLabel?: string;
    80	}) {
    81	  const iconColor =
    82	    icon?.title === "Gradle"
    83	      ? "#9fd7dc"
    84	      : icon?.title === "PayPal"
    85	        ? "#35bdf2"
    86	      : icon?.hex === "000000" || icon?.hex === "1C2024"
    87	      ? "#f3fff7"
    88	      : icon
    89	        ? `#${icon.hex}`
    90	        : undefined;
    91	
    92	  return (
    93	    <li>
    94	      {imageSrc ? (
    95	        <Image
    96	          alt={imageAlt ?? ""}
    97	          className="capability-brand-mark"
    98	          height={24}
    99	          src={imageSrc}
   100	          width={24}
   101	        />
   102	      ) : postmark ? (
   103	        <svg
   104	          aria-label="Postmark logo"
   105	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   106	          role="img"
   107	          viewBox="0 0 30 30"
   108	        >
   109	          <path
   110	            clipRule="evenodd"
   111	            d="M30 27.422v1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 28.828 30h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0h-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0-2.344 0H1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 28.828v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344v-1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V8.672a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V4.922a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0-2.344V1.172A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 1.172 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 2.344 0h1.406A1.172 1.172 0 0 0 30 1.172v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344v1.406a1.172 1.172 0 0 0 0 2.344Z"
   112	            fill="#ffde00"
   113	            fillRule="evenodd"
   114	          />
   115	          <path
   116	            d="M8.703 21.38h.935c.481 0 .784-.31.784-.8V9.628c0-.49-.303-.8-.784-.8h-.935V6.4h6.775C19.118 6.4 22 8.286 22 11.876c0 3.616-2.882 5.501-6.522 5.501h-2.326v3.203c0 .49.304.8.81.8h1.895v2.454H8.703V21.38Zm6.573-6.534c2.401 0 3.792-1.033 3.792-2.918 0-1.938-1.39-2.919-3.792-2.919h-2.124v5.863h2.124v-.026Z"
   117	            fill="#000"
   118	          />
   119	        </svg>
   120	      ) : Symbol ? (
   121	        <Symbol
   122	          aria-label={symbolLabel ?? `${label} symbol`}
   123	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   124	          role="img"
   125	        />
   126	      ) : icon ? (
   127	        <svg
   128	          aria-label={`${icon.title} logo`}
   129	          className="capability-brand-mark"
   130	          role="img"
   131	          style={{ color: iconColor }}
   132	          viewBox={iconStroke ? "-1 -1 26 26" : "0 0 24 24"}
   133	        >
   134	          <path
   135	            d={icon.path}
   136	            fill="currentColor"
   137	            paintOrder={iconStroke ? "stroke fill" : undefined}
   138	            stroke={iconStroke}
   139	            strokeLinejoin={iconStroke ? "round" : undefined}
   140	            strokeWidth={iconStroke ? 1.1 : undefined}
   141	          />
   142	        </svg>
   143	      ) : (
   144	        <span className="capability-brand-spacer" aria-hidden="true" />
   145	      )}
   146	      <span>{label}</span>
   147	    </li>
   148	  );
   149	}

   960	function PhonePreview({
   961	  title,
   962	  metric,
   963	  variant,
   964	  href,
   965	  screenImageSrc,
   966	  screenImageAlt,
   967	}: {
   968	  href?: string;
   969	  title: string;
   970	  metric: string;
   971	  variant: "commerce" | "finance";
   972	  screenImageSrc?: string;
   973	  screenImageAlt?: string;
   974	}) {
   975	  const className = `phone-shell phone-shell-${variant}${
   976	    screenImageSrc ? " phone-shell-capture" : ""
   977	  }${href ? " phone-shell-link" : ""}`;
   978	  const content = screenImageSrc ? (
   979	    <Image
   980	      alt={screenImageAlt ?? `${title} app screen`}
   981	      className="phone-framed-image"
   982	      fill
   983	      priority
   984	      sizes="245px"
   985	      src={screenImageSrc}
   986	    />
   987	  ) : (
   988	    <>
   989	      <div className="phone-speaker" />
   990	      <div className="phone-screen">
   991	        <div className="phone-status">
   992	          <span>{title}</span>
   993	          <span>5G</span>
   994	        </div>
   995	        <div className="phone-chart">
   996	          <div />
   997	          <div />
   998	          <div />
   999	          <div />
  1000	        </div>
  1001	        <div className="phone-cardline wide" />
  1002	        <div className="phone-cardline" />
  1003	        <div className="phone-grid">
  1004	          <span />
  1005	          <span />
  1006	          <span />
  1007	          <span />
  1008	        </div>
  1009	        <div className="phone-cta">{metric}</div>
  1010	      </div>
  1011	    </>
  1012	  );
  1013	
  1014	  if (href) {
  1015	    return (
  1016	      <a
  1017	        aria-label={`Open ${title} case study`}
  1018	        className={className}
  1019	        href={href}
  1020	      >
  1021	        {content}
  1022	      </a>
  1023	    );
  1024	  }
  1025	
  1026	  return <div className={className}>{content}</div>;
  1027	}
  1028

  1042	        src="/images/cinerific-hero-tablet-tab-s7-uniform.png"
  1043	      />
  1044	    </a>
  1045	  );
  1046	}

    74	export function ProjectCtaTextSnippet({
    75	  caseStudyId,
    76	  className = "",
    77	}: {
    78	  caseStudyId: string;
    79	  className?: string;
    80	}) {
    81	  const project = projectLinks.find(
    82	    (projectLink) => projectLink.caseStudyId === caseStudyId,
    83	  );
    84	
    85	  if (!project) {
    86	    return null;
    87	  }
    88	
    89	  return (
    90	    <span
    91	      className={`carousel-button-action carousel-button-action-${project.tone}${className ? ` ${className}` : ""}`}
    92	      style={{
    93	        ...strokedWhiteTextStyle,
    94	        alignItems: "center",
    95	        color: projectNameColors[project.tone],
    96	        display: "inline-flex",
    97	        flexDirection: "column",
    98	        fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-name-font-size)",
    99	        gap: "2.2px",
   100	        justifyContent: "center",
   101	        lineHeight: 0.95,
   102	      }}
   103	    >
   104	      <span
   105	        className="carousel-button-action-name"
   106	        style={{ fontSize: project.nameFontSize }}
   107	      >
   108	        {project.name}
   109	      </span>
   110	      <span
   111	        className="carousel-button-action-category"
   112	        style={{
   113	          WebkitTextStroke: 0,
   114	          color: "#000",
   115	          fontSize: "var(--hero-carousel-category-font-size)",
   116	          fontWeight: 900,
   117	          letterSpacing: "0.06em",
   118	          lineHeight: 1,
   119	          textAlign: "center",
   120	          textShadow: "none",
   121	        }}
   122	      >
   123	        {project.category}
   124	      </span>
   125	    </span>
   126	  );
   127	}
   128	
   129	function getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset() {
   130	  const isWideLandscape = window.matchMedia(
   131	    "(min-width: 1024px) and (orientation: landscape)",
   132	  ).matches;
   133	  const isPortrait = window.matchMedia("(orientation: portrait)").matches;
   134	
   135	  if (isWideLandscape) {
   136	    return Math.max(18, Math.min(44, window.innerHeight * 0.035));
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  if (isPortrait) {
   140	    return Math.max(14, Math.min(36, window.innerHeight * 0.03));
   141	  }
   142	
   143	  return Math.max(16, Math.min(40, window.innerHeight * 0.0325));
   144	}
   145	
   146	function scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(caseStudyId: string) {
   147	  const targetCard = document.getElementById(caseStudyId);
   148	
   149	  if (!targetCard) {
   150	    return;
   151	  }
   152	
   153	  const caseGrid = targetCard.closest<HTMLElement>(".case-grid");
   154	  const targetTop = targetCard.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   155	  const rowCards = caseGrid
   156	    ? Array.from(caseGrid.children).filter((child): child is HTMLElement => {
   157	        if (!(child instanceof HTMLElement)) {
   158	          return false;
   159	        }
   160	
   161	        const cardTop = child.getBoundingClientRect().top;
   162	
   163	        return Math.abs(cardTop - targetTop) <= 8;
   164	      })
   165	    : [targetCard];
   166	  const rowTop = Math.min(
   167	    ...rowCards.map((card) => card.getBoundingClientRect().top),
   168	  );
   169	
   170	  window.scrollTo({
   171	    behavior: "smooth",
   172	    top: window.scrollY + rowTop - getCaseStudyRowScrollOffset(),
   173	  });
   174	
   175	  window.history.replaceState(null, "", `#${caseStudyId}`);
   176	}

   193	      preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
   194	      width={androidLogoSize.width}
   195	      x={x}
   196	      y={y}
   197	    />
   198	  );
   199	}

   212	      preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
   213	      width={iosLogoSize.width}

   215	      y={y}
   216	    />
   217	  );

   275	          />
   276	        );
   277	      })}
   278	    </svg>
   279	  );
   280	}

   352	const platformLabelColumnStart = 3;
   353	
   354	function getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index: number) {
   355	  return platformLabelColumnStart + index * 2;
   356	}
   357	
   358	function getPlatformDividerGridColumn(index: number) {
   359	  return getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index) + 1;
   360	}
   361	
   362	function getPlatformListGridTemplate(columnCount: number) {
   363	  const columns = [
   364	    "var(--case-platform-heading-column-width, 92px)",
   365	    "minmax(8px, 1fr)",
   366	  ];
   367	
   368	  Array.from({ length: columnCount }).forEach((_, index) => {
   369	    columns.push("max-content");
   370	
   371	    if (index < columnCount - 1) {
   372	      columns.push("0px");
   373	    }
   374	  });
   375	
   376	  return columns.join(" ");
   377	}
   378	
   379	function PlatformIconSlot({
   380	  brandMark,
   381	  deviceMark,
   382	  gridColumn,
   383	  gridRow,
   384	  label,
   385	  slotIndex,
   386	}: {
   387	  brandMark?: "android" | "apple" | "web";
   388	  deviceMark?: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   389	  gridColumn?: number;
   390	  gridRow?: number;
   391	  label: string;
   392	  slotIndex?: number;
   393	}) {
   394	  return (
   395	    <span
   396	      className="case-card-title-platform-slot"
   397	      data-platform-brand-mark={brandMark}
   398	      data-platform-device-mark={deviceMark}
   399	      data-platform-index={slotIndex}
   400	      data-platform-label={brandMark || deviceMark ? "" : label}
   401	      data-platform-row={gridRow}
   402	      style={
   403	        gridColumn && gridRow
   404	          ? {
   405	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   406	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   407	            }
   408	          : undefined
   409	      }
   410	    >
   411	      {deviceMark ? (
   412	        <FactorDeviceMark device={deviceMark} />
   413	      ) : brandMark === "web" ? (
   414	        <WebMark scale={0.72} />
   415	      ) : brandMark ? (
   416	        <img
   417	          alt=""
   418	          aria-hidden="true"
   419	          className={`case-card-title-platform-brand-mark case-card-title-platform-brand-mark-${brandMark}`}
   420	          height={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   421	          src={
   422	            brandMark === "android"
   423	              ? "/images/android-robot-head.svg"
   424	              : "/images/apple-logo-official.svg"
   425	          }
   426	          style={{
   427	            height: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   428	            maxHeight: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   429	            maxWidth: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   430	            width: brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16,
   431	          }}
   432	          width={brandMark === "android" ? 17 : 16}
   433	        />
   434	      ) : null}
   435	    </span>
   436	  );
   437	}
   438	
   439	function PlatformLabelDivider({
   440	  className,
   441	  gridColumn,
   442	  gridRow,
   443	  kind,
   444	}: {
   445	  className?: string;
   446	  gridColumn?: number;
   447	  gridRow?: number | string;
   448	  kind: "grid" | "item";
   449	}) {
   450	  const dividerClassName = [
   451	    `case-card-title-platform-divider case-card-title-platform-${kind}-divider`,
   452	    className,
   453	  ]
   454	    .filter(Boolean)
   455	    .join(" ");
   456	
   457	  return (
   458	    <span
   459	      aria-hidden="true"
   460	      className={dividerClassName}
   461	      style={
   462	        gridColumn && gridRow
   463	          ? {
   464	              gridColumn: String(gridColumn),
   465	              gridRow: String(gridRow),
   466	            }
   467	          : undefined
   468	      }
   469	    />
   470	  );
   471	}
   472	
   473	function PlatformLabelSlots({
   474	  useDeviceMarks,
   475	  useBrandMarks,
   476	  labels,
   477	  row,
   478	}: {
   479	  useBrandMarks?: boolean;
   480	  useDeviceMarks?: boolean;
   481	  labels: string[];
   482	  row: number;
   483	}) {
   484	  return labels.map((label, index) => (
   485	    <PlatformIconSlot
   486	      gridColumn={getPlatformLabelGridColumn(index)}
   487	      gridRow={row}
   488	      key={`${row}-${label}`}
   489	      label={label}
   490	      brandMark={
   491	        useBrandMarks
   492	          ? label === "AND"
   493	            ? "android"
   494	            : label === "IOS"
   495	              ? "apple"
   496	              : label === "WEB"
   497	                ? "web"
   498	                : undefined
   499	          : undefined
   500	      }
   501	      deviceMark={
   502	        useDeviceMarks
   503	          ? label === "PHONE"
   504	              ? "phone"
   505	            : label === "TABLET"
   506	              ? "tablet"
   507	              : label === "DESK"
   508	                ? "desktop"
   509	                : undefined
   510	          : undefined
   511	      }
   512	      slotIndex={index}
   513	    />
   514	  ));
   515	}
   516	
   517	function PlatformColumnDividers({
   518	  columnCount,
   519	  gridRow,
   520	  partial,
   521	  startIndex = 0,
   522	}: {
   523	  columnCount: number;
   524	  gridRow: number | string;
   525	  partial?: boolean;
   526	  startIndex?: number;
   527	}) {
   528	  return Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, columnCount - 1 - startIndex) }).map(
   529	    (_, index) => {
   530	      const dividerIndex = startIndex + index;
   531	
   532	      return (
   533	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   534	          className={
   535	            partial
   536	              ? "case-card-title-platform-partial-divider"
   537	              : undefined
   538	          }
   539	          gridColumn={getPlatformDividerGridColumn(dividerIndex)}
   540	          gridRow={gridRow}
   541	          key={`divider-${gridRow}-${dividerIndex}`}
   542	          kind="item"
   543	        />
   544	      );
   545	    },
   546	  );
   547	}

   611	function PlatformLogoMark({ platform }: { platform: MobilePlatform }) {

   615	    <ApplePlatformMark />

   619	function FactorDeviceMark({
   620	  device,
   621	}: {
   622	  device: "phone" | "tablet" | "desktop";
   623	}) {
   624	  if (device === "phone") {
   625	    return <PhoneMark mobilePlatforms={[]} />;
   626	  }
   627	
   628	  if (device === "tablet") {
   629	    return <TabletMark showPlatformIcon={false} />;
   630	  }
   631	
   632	  return (
   633	    <img
   634	      alt=""
   635	      aria-hidden="true"
   636	      className="case-card-title-desktop-mark"
   637	      src="/images/freeicons-chip-component-white.svg"
   638	    />
   639	  );
   640	}
   641	
   642	export function ProjectDeviceStack({
   643	  assetGap,
   644	  color,
   645	  hasTablet,
   646	  hasWeb,
   647	  deviceLabels,
   648	  mobilePlatforms,
   649	  platformLabels: customPlatformLabels,
   650	  usePlatformBrandMarks,
   651	  responsiveAssetGap,
   652	  showPlatformLabels,
   653	  webIconScale,
   654	}: {
   655	  assetGap?: string;
   656	  color: string;
   657	  deviceLabels?: string[];
   658	  hasTablet?: boolean;
   659	  hasWeb?: boolean;
   660	  mobilePlatforms: MobilePlatform[];
   661	  platformLabels?: string[];
   662	  usePlatformBrandMarks?: boolean;
   663	  responsiveAssetGap?: ResponsiveAssetGap;
   664	  showPlatformLabels?: boolean;
   665	  webIconScale?: number;
   666	}) {
   667	  const stackRef = useRef<HTMLSpanElement>(null);
   668	  const [measuredAssetGap, setMeasuredAssetGap] = useState<string | null>(null);
   669	  const mobilePlatformKey = mobilePlatforms.join(",");
   670	  const stackStyle: CSSProperties & {
   671	    "--project-device-stack-gap"?: string;
   672	  } = {
   673	    color,
   674	  };
   675	  const stackGap = measuredAssetGap ?? assetGap;
   676	  const deviceLabelKey = deviceLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   677	  const platformLabelKey = customPlatformLabels?.join(",") ?? "";
   678	
   679	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   680	    if (!responsiveAssetGap) {
   681	      setMeasuredAssetGap(null);
   682	      return;
   683	    }
   684	
   685	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   686	    const measurementTarget = stack?.parentElement ?? stack;
   687	
   688	    if (!measurementTarget) {
   689	      return;
   690	    }
   691	
   692	    let frame = 0;
   693	    const updateGap = () => {
   694	      if (frame) {
   695	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   696	      }
   697	
   698	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   699	        const laneWidth = measurementTarget.getBoundingClientRect().width;
   700	        const activeRange = Math.max(
   701	          1,
   702	          responsiveAssetGap.wideLanePx - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx,
   703	        );
   704	        const progress = Math.min(
   705	          1,
   706	          Math.max(
   707	            0,
   708	            (laneWidth - responsiveAssetGap.tightLanePx) / activeRange,
   709	          ),
   710	        );
   711	        const nextGap =
   712	          responsiveAssetGap.minPx +
   713	          (responsiveAssetGap.maxPx - responsiveAssetGap.minPx) * progress;
   714	        const nextGapValue = `${nextGap.toFixed(2)}px`;
   715	
   716	        setMeasuredAssetGap((currentGap) =>
   717	          currentGap === nextGapValue ? currentGap : nextGapValue,
   718	        );
   719	      });
   720	    };
   721	
   722	    updateGap();
   723	
   724	    const resizeObserver =
   725	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   726	        ? null
   727	        : new ResizeObserver(updateGap);
   728	
   729	    resizeObserver?.observe(measurementTarget);
   730	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   731	
   732	    return () => {
   733	      if (frame) {
   734	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   735	      }
   736	
   737	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   738	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateGap);
   739	    };
   740	  }, [
   741	    responsiveAssetGap?.maxPx,
   742	    responsiveAssetGap?.minPx,
   743	    responsiveAssetGap?.tightLanePx,
   744	    responsiveAssetGap?.wideLanePx,
   745	  ]);
   746	
   747	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
   748	    if (!showPlatformLabels) {
   749	      return;
   750	    }
   751	
   752	    const stack = stackRef.current;
   753	    const rowDivider = stack?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   754	      ".case-card-title-platform-row-divider",
   755	    );
   756	
   757	    if (!stack || !rowDivider) {
   758	      return;
   759	    }
   760	
   761	    let frame = 0;
   762	    const updateRowDivider = () => {
   763	      if (frame) {
   764	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   765	      }
   766	
   767	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
   768	        const platformHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   769	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-platform",
   770	        );
   771	        const deviceHeading = stack.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
   772	          ".case-card-title-platform-heading-factor",
   773	        );
   774	        const verticalDividers = Array.from(
   775	          stack.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
   776	            ".case-card-title-platform-divider",
   777	          ),
   778	        );
   779	
   780	        if (
   781	          !platformHeading ||
   782	          !deviceHeading ||
   783	          verticalDividers.length === 0
   784	        ) {
   785	          stack.removeAttribute("data-platform-row-divider-ready");
   786	          return;
   787	        }
   788	
   789	        const stackRect = stack.getBoundingClientRect();
   790	        const platformRect = platformHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   791	        const deviceRect = deviceHeading.getBoundingClientRect();
   792	        const dividerRects = verticalDividers.map((divider) =>
   793	          divider.getBoundingClientRect(),
   794	        );
   795	        const leftmostDividerCenter = Math.min(
   796	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   797	        );
   798	        const rightmostDividerCenter = Math.max(
   799	          ...dividerRects.map((rect) => rect.left + rect.width / 2),
   800	        );
   801	        const dividerStartCenter = deviceRect.left + deviceRect.width / 2;
   802	        const dividerOverhang = Math.max(
   803	          0,
   804	          leftmostDividerCenter - dividerStartCenter,
   805	        );
   806	        const dividerEndCenter = rightmostDividerCenter + dividerOverhang;
   807	        const dividerStart = dividerStartCenter - stackRect.left;
   808	        const dividerEnd = dividerEndCenter - stackRect.left;
   809	        const dividerTop =
   810	          (platformRect.top +
   811	            platformRect.height / 2 +
   812	            deviceRect.top +
   813	            deviceRect.height / 2) /
   814	            2 -
   815	          stackRect.top;
   816	        const dividerWidth = Math.max(0, dividerEnd - dividerStart);
   817	
   818	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   819	          "--case-platform-row-divider-left",
   820	          `${dividerStart.toFixed(2)}px`,
   821	        );
   822	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   823	          "--case-platform-row-divider-top",
   824	          `${dividerTop.toFixed(2)}px`,
   825	        );
   826	        rowDivider.style.setProperty(
   827	          "--case-platform-row-divider-width",
   828	          `${dividerWidth.toFixed(2)}px`,
   829	        );
   830	        stack.toggleAttribute(
   831	          "data-platform-row-divider-ready",
   832	          dividerWidth > 0,
   833	        );
   834	      });
   835	    };
   836	
   837	    updateRowDivider();
   838	    document.fonts?.ready.then(updateRowDivider);
   839	
   840	    const resizeObserver =
   841	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
   842	        ? null
   843	        : new ResizeObserver(updateRowDivider);
   844	
   845	    resizeObserver?.observe(stack);
   846	    window.addEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   847	
   848	    return () => {
   849	      if (frame) {
   850	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
   851	      }
   852	
   853	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
   854	      window.removeEventListener("resize", updateRowDivider);
   855	    };
   856	  }, [
   857	    deviceLabelKey,
   858	    hasTablet,
   859	    hasWeb,
   860	    mobilePlatformKey,
   861	    platformLabelKey,
   862	    showPlatformLabels,
   863	    stackGap,
   864	  ]);
   865	
   866	  if (stackGap) {
   867	    stackStyle["--project-device-stack-gap"] = stackGap;
   868	  }
   869	
   870	  if (showPlatformLabels) {
   871	    const platforms: Array<MobilePlatform | "web"> = [];
   872	    const factorDevices: Array<"phone" | "tablet"> = [];
   873	
   874	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("android") || hasTablet) {
   875	      platforms.push("android");
   876	    }
   877	
   878	    if (mobilePlatforms.includes("apple")) {
   879	      platforms.push("apple");
   880	    }
   881	
   882	    if (hasWeb) {
   883	      platforms.push("web");
   884	    }
   885	
   886	    if (mobilePlatforms.length > 0) {
   887	      factorDevices.push("phone");
   888	    }
   889	
   890	    if (hasTablet) {
   891	      factorDevices.push("tablet");
   892	    }
   893	
   894	    const platformLabels =
   895	      customPlatformLabels ??
   896	      platforms.map((platform) =>
   897	        platform === "web" ? "WEB" : platform === "android" ? "AND" : "IOS",
   898	      );
   899	    const factorDeviceLabels =
   900	      deviceLabels ??
   901	      factorDevices.map((device) =>
   902	        device === "phone" ? "PHONE" : "TABLET",
   903	      );
   904	    const labelColumnCount = Math.max(
   905	      platformLabels.length,
   906	      factorDeviceLabels.length,
   907	      1,
   908	    );
   909	    const dividerGridRow = factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? "1 / 3" : 1;
   910	    const spanningItemDividerCount =
   911	      factorDeviceLabels.length > platformLabels.length
   912	        ? platformLabels.length
   913	        : Math.max(0, platformLabels.length - 1);
   914	    const platformListStyle = {
   915	      ...stackStyle,
   916	      gridTemplateColumns: getPlatformListGridTemplate(labelColumnCount),
   917	    };
   918	
   919	    return (
   920	      <span
   921	        className="carousel-button-project case-card-title-platform-list"
   922	        ref={stackRef}
   923	        style={platformListStyle}
   924	      >
   925	        <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-platform">
   926	          PLATFORM
   927	        </span>
   928	        <PlatformLabelDivider
   929	          gridColumn={2}
   930	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   931	          kind="grid"
   932	        />
   933	        <PlatformLabelSlots
   934	          labels={platformLabels}
   935	          row={1}
   936	          useBrandMarks={usePlatformBrandMarks}
   937	        />
   938	        <PlatformColumnDividers
   939	          columnCount={spanningItemDividerCount + 1}
   940	          gridRow={dividerGridRow}
   941	        />
   942	        {factorDeviceLabels.length > 0 ? (
   943	          <>
   944	            <span className="case-card-title-platform-heading case-card-title-platform-heading-factor">
   945	              DEVICE
   946	            </span>
   947	            <PlatformLabelSlots
   948	              labels={factorDeviceLabels}
   949	              row={2}
   950	              useDeviceMarks
   951	            />
   952	            <PlatformColumnDividers
   953	              columnCount={factorDeviceLabels.length}
   954	              gridRow={2}
   955	              partial
   956	              startIndex={spanningItemDividerCount}
   957	            />
   958	            <span
   959	              aria-hidden="true"
   960	              className="case-card-title-platform-row-divider"
   961	            />
   962	          </>
   963	        ) : null}
   964	      </span>
   965	    );
   966	  }
   967	
   968	  return (
   969	    <span className="carousel-button-project" ref={stackRef} style={stackStyle}>
   970	      {mobilePlatforms.map((platform) => (
   971	        <PhoneMark key={platform} mobilePlatforms={[platform]} />
   972	      ))}
   973	      {hasTablet ? <TabletMark /> : null}
   974	      {hasWeb ? <WebMark scale={webIconScale} /> : null}
   975	    </span>
   976	  );
   977	}
   978	
   979	function CarouselArrowMark() {
   980	  return (
   981	    <svg
   982	      aria-hidden
   983	      className="carousel-button-arrow-mark"
   984	      viewBox="0 0 32 24"
   985	      xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"
   986	    >
   987	      <path
   988	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   989	        fill="none"
   990	        stroke="#000"
   991	        strokeLinecap="round"
   992	        strokeLinejoin="round"
   993	        strokeWidth="6.2"
   994	      />
   995	      <path
   996	        d="M8.4 12H26M18 4l8 8-8 8"
   997	        fill="none"
   998	        stroke="#f3fff7"
   999	        strokeLinecap="round"
  1000	        strokeLinejoin="round"
  1001	        strokeWidth="3.6"
  1002	      />
  1003	    </svg>
  1004	  );
  1005	}
  1006	
  1007	export function ProjectCarouselButton() {
  1008	  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);
  1009	  const projectButtonRef = useRef<HTMLAnchorElement>(null);
  1010	  const activeProject = projectLinks[activeIndex];
  1011	
  1012	  const handleArrowClick = (event: MouseEvent<HTMLAnchorElement>) => {
  1013	    event.preventDefault();
  1014	    scrollCaseStudyRowIntoView(activeProject.caseStudyId);
  1015	  };
  1016	
  1017	  useEffect(() => {
  1018	    const interval = window.setInterval(() => {
  1019	      setActiveIndex((currentIndex) => (currentIndex + 1) % projectLinks.length);
  1020	    }, 5000);
  1021	
  1022	    return () => window.clearInterval(interval);
  1023	  }, []);
  1024	
  1025	  useLayoutEffect(() => {
  1026	    let frame = 0;
  1027	
  1028	    const syncHeroActionGeometry = () => {
  1029	      if (frame) {
  1030	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1031	      }
  1032	
  1033	      frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(() => {
  1034	        const heroCopy = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-copy");
  1035	        const heroActions =
  1036	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1037	        const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1038	        const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1039	        const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1040	        const phoneStage = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".phone-stage");
  1041	        const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1042	        const profilePhoto =
  1043	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1044	        const socialButton =
  1045	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-social-button");
  1046	        const visualFrameSelectors = [
  1047	          ".profile-photo",
  1048	          ".phone-shell-commerce",
  1049	          ".phone-shell-finance",
  1050	          ".tablet-shell",
  1051	        ];
  1052	
  1053	        if (proofPanel && profilePhoto) {
  1054	          const proofRect = proofPanel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1055	          const profileRect = profilePhoto.getBoundingClientRect();
  1056	          const proofWidth = profileRect.right - proofRect.left;
  1057	
  1058	          if (proofWidth > 0) {
  1059	            [heroCopy, proofPanel, stackRow].forEach((element) => {
  1060	              element?.style.setProperty(
  1061	                "--hero-proof-landscape-width",
  1062	                `${proofWidth}px`,
  1063	              );
  1064	            });
  1065	          }
  1066	        }
  1067	
  1068	        if (heroVisual && navBar && stackRow) {
  1069	          const isTwoColumnHero = window.matchMedia(
  1070	            "(min-width: 1041px), (min-width: 981px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1071	          ).matches;
  1072	
  1073	          if (!isTwoColumnHero) {
  1074	            heroVisual.style.removeProperty("--hero-visual-center-offset");
  1075	          } else if (window.scrollY <= 1) {
  1076	            const isCompactLandscapeHero = window.matchMedia(
  1077	              "(min-width: 981px) and (max-width: 1023px) and (orientation: landscape)",
  1078	            ).matches;
  1079	            const heroVisualScale =
  1080	              heroVisual.offsetWidth > 0
  1081	                ? heroVisual.getBoundingClientRect().width / heroVisual.offsetWidth
  1082	                : 1;
  1083	            const deviceStageSnap = isCompactLandscapeHero
  1084	              ? (Number.parseFloat(
  1085	                  window
  1086	                    .getComputedStyle(phoneStage ?? heroVisual)
  1087	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-device-stage-snap"),
  1088	                ) || 0) * heroVisualScale
  1089	              : 0;
  1090	            const visualRects = visualFrameSelectors
  1091	              .map((selector) => ({
  1092	                element: document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(selector),
  1093	                selector,
  1094	              }))
  1095	              .filter(
  1096	                (
  1097	                  entry,
  1098	                ): entry is { element: HTMLElement; selector: string } =>
  1099	                  Boolean(entry.element),
  1100	              )
  1101	              .map(({ element, selector }) => {
  1102	                const rect = element.getBoundingClientRect();
  1103	                const snapAdjustment =
  1104	                  selector === ".profile-photo" ? 0 : deviceStageSnap;
  1105	
  1106	                return {
  1107	                  bottom: rect.bottom - snapAdjustment,
  1108	                  top: rect.top - snapAdjustment,
  1109	                };
  1110	              });
  1111	
  1112	            if (visualRects.length > 0) {
  1113	              const navRect = navBar.getBoundingClientRect();
  1114	              const stackRect = stackRow.getBoundingClientRect();
  1115	              const visualTop = Math.min(
  1116	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.top),
  1117	              );
  1118	              const visualBottom = Math.max(
  1119	                ...visualRects.map((rect) => rect.bottom),
  1120	              );
  1121	              const currentOffset =
  1122	                Number.parseFloat(
  1123	                  window
  1124	                    .getComputedStyle(heroVisual)
  1125	                    .getPropertyValue("--hero-visual-center-offset"),
  1126	                ) || 0;
  1127	              const visualCenterWithoutOffset =
  1128	                (visualTop + visualBottom) / 2 - currentOffset;
  1129	              const targetCenter = (navRect.bottom + stackRect.top) / 2;
  1130	              const nextOffset = targetCenter - visualCenterWithoutOffset;
  1131	
  1132	              heroVisual.style.setProperty(
  1133	                "--hero-visual-center-offset",
  1134	                `${nextOffset}px`,
  1135	              );
  1136	            }
  1137	          }
  1138	        }
  1139	
  1140	        if (heroActions && socialButton) {
  1141	          const heroActionsWidth = heroActions.getBoundingClientRect().width;
  1142	          const socialButtonHeight =
  1143	            socialButton.getBoundingClientRect().height;
  1144	
  1145	          if (heroCopy && heroActionsWidth > 0) {
  1146	            heroCopy.style.setProperty(
  1147	              "--hero-text-stack-width",
  1148	              `${heroActionsWidth}px`,
  1149	            );
  1150	          }
  1151	
  1152	          if (socialButtonHeight > 0) {
  1153	            const setPx = (name: string, value: number) => {
  1154	              heroActions.style.setProperty(name, `${value}px`);
  1155	            };
  1156	
  1157	            setPx("--hero-social-button-height", socialButtonHeight);
  1158	            setPx("--hero-social-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.295833);
  1159	            setPx("--hero-social-icon-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.672);
  1160	            const baseSocialButtonHeight = 48;
  1161	            const baseSocialGap = baseSocialButtonHeight * 0.045833 + 1;
  1162	            const stretchedSocialGap =
  1163	              Math.max(0, socialButtonHeight - baseSocialButtonHeight) *
  1164	              0.091666;
  1165	
  1166	            setPx("--hero-social-gap", baseSocialGap + stretchedSocialGap);
  1167	            setPx("--hero-social-pad-x", socialButtonHeight / 8);
  1168	            setPx("--hero-view-label-font-size", socialButtonHeight * 0.275);
  1169	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1170	              "--hero-eyebrow-font-size",
  1171	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.367}px`,
  1172	            );
  1173	            heroCopy?.style.setProperty(
  1174	              "--hero-lede-font-size",
  1175	              `${socialButtonHeight * 0.5}px`,
  1176	            );
  1177	            setPx(
  1178	              "--hero-carousel-base-font-size",
  1179	              socialButtonHeight * 0.34375,
  1180	            );
  1181	            setPx(
  1182	              "--hero-carousel-name-font-size",
  1183	              socialButtonHeight * 0.352917,
  1184	            );
  1185	            setPx(
  1186	              "--hero-carousel-category-font-size",
  1187	              socialButtonHeight * 0.176458,
  1188	            );
  1189	            setPx(
  1190	              "--hero-carousel-arrow-font-size",
  1191	              socialButtonHeight * 0.458333,
  1192	            );
  1193	          }
  1194	        }
  1195	
  1196	        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("hero-stack-sync"));
  1197	
  1198	        const viewLabel =
  1199	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1200	        const githubText =
  1201	          document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1202	        const githubIcon =
  1203	          document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1204	
  1205	        if (viewLabel && githubText && githubIcon) {
  1206	          const viewLabelRect = viewLabel.getBoundingClientRect();
  1207	          const githubTextRect = githubText.getBoundingClientRect();
  1208	          const githubIconRect = githubIcon.getBoundingClientRect();
  1209	          const clusterLeft = Math.min(githubTextRect.left, githubIconRect.left);
  1210	          const stackLeft = clusterLeft - viewLabelRect.left;
  1211	
  1212	          viewLabel.style.setProperty(
  1213	            "--hero-view-stack-left",
  1214	            `${stackLeft}px`,
  1215	          );
  1216	        }
  1217	      });
  1218	    };
  1219	
  1220	    syncHeroActionGeometry();
  1221	    window.addEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1222	
  1223	    const targetIcon =
  1224	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".dribbble-icon-mark") ??
  1225	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".linkedin-icon-mark");
  1226	    const navBar = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".nav-bar");
  1227	    const heroVisual = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-visual");
  1228	    const heroActions = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-actions");
  1229	    const stackRow = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".stack-row");
  1230	    const proofPanel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".proof-panel");
  1231	    const profilePhoto = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".profile-photo");
  1232	    const targetButton = targetIcon?.closest(".hero-social-button");
  1233	    const viewLabel = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".hero-view-label");
  1234	    const githubText = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".github-label-text");
  1235	    const githubIcon =
  1236	      document.querySelector<SVGSVGElement>(".github-icon-mark");
  1237	    const githubButton = githubText?.closest(".github-button");
  1238	    const resizeObserver =
  1239	      typeof ResizeObserver === "undefined"
  1240	        ? null
  1241	        : new ResizeObserver(syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1242	
  1243	    if (resizeObserver) {
  1244	      if (projectButtonRef.current) {
  1245	        resizeObserver.observe(projectButtonRef.current);
  1246	      }
  1247	
  1248	      if (heroActions) {
  1249	        resizeObserver.observe(heroActions);
  1250	      }
  1251	
  1252	      if (heroVisual) {
  1253	        resizeObserver.observe(heroVisual);
  1254	      }
  1255	
  1256	      if (stackRow) {
  1257	        resizeObserver.observe(stackRow);
  1258	      }
  1259	
  1260	      if (proofPanel) {
  1261	        resizeObserver.observe(proofPanel);
  1262	      }
  1263	
  1264	      if (profilePhoto) {
  1265	        resizeObserver.observe(profilePhoto);
  1266	      }
  1267	
  1268	      if (targetIcon) {
  1269	        resizeObserver.observe(targetIcon);
  1270	      }
  1271	
  1272	      if (targetButton) {
  1273	        resizeObserver.observe(targetButton);
  1274	      }
  1275	
  1276	      if (viewLabel) {
  1277	        resizeObserver.observe(viewLabel);
  1278	      }
  1279	
  1280	      if (githubText) {
  1281	        resizeObserver.observe(githubText);
  1282	      }
  1283	
  1284	      if (githubIcon) {
  1285	        resizeObserver.observe(githubIcon);
  1286	      }
  1287	
  1288	      if (githubButton) {
  1289	        resizeObserver.observe(githubButton);
  1290	      }
  1291	    }
  1292	
  1293	    if ("fonts" in document) {
  1294	      document.fonts.ready.then(syncHeroActionGeometry).catch(() => {});
  1295	    }
  1296	
  1297	    return () => {
  1298	      if (frame) {
  1299	        window.cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
  1300	      }
  1301	
  1302	      window.removeEventListener("resize", syncHeroActionGeometry);
  1303	      resizeObserver?.disconnect();
  1304	    };
  1305	  }, []);
  1306	
  1307	  return (
  1308	    <span className="carousel-button-group">
  1309	      <a
  1310	        aria-label={`Open ${activeProject.name} project`}
  1311	        className="button button-primary carousel-button"
  1312	        href={activeProject.href}
  1313	        ref={projectButtonRef}
  1314	        rel="noreferrer"
  1315	        target="_blank"
  1316	      >
  1317	        <span className="carousel-button-window" aria-live="polite">
  1318	          <span className="carousel-button-label" key={activeProject.href}>
  1319	            <ProjectCtaTextSnippet caseStudyId={activeProject.caseStudyId} />
  1320	            <ProjectDeviceStack
  1321	              color={projectNameColors[activeProject.tone]}
  1322	              hasTablet={activeProject.hasTablet}
  1323	              hasWeb={activeProject.hasWeb}
  1324	              mobilePlatforms={activeProject.mobilePlatforms}
  1325	            />
  1326	          </span>
  1327	        </span>
  1328	      </a>
  1329	      <a
  1330	        aria-label={`Scroll to ${activeProject.name} case study`}
  1331	        className="button button-primary carousel-arrow-button"
  1332	        href={`#${activeProject.caseStudyId}`}
  1333	        onClick={handleArrowClick}
  1334	      >
  1335	        <span aria-hidden className="carousel-button-arrow">
  1336	          <CarouselArrowMark />
  1337	        </span>
  1338	      </a>
  1339	    </span>
  1340	  );
  1341	}

============================================================
FILE: README.md
============================================================

     1	# Prahl.dev Portfolio Site
     2	
     3	Next.js + Tailwind CSS portfolio start page for Prahl.dev.
     4	
     5	## What is included
     6	
     7	- `app/page.tsx` - the homepage/start page.
     8	- `app/globals.css` - the green/black visual system and responsive layout.
     9	- `public/images/profile-placeholder.png` - generated AI profile placeholder; replace this with your own square photo later using the same filename.
    10	- `public/images/start-page-mockup-reference.png` - the original generated mockup reference.
    11	- `public/favicon.svg` - simple green/black monogram favicon.
    12	
    13	## Local development
    14	
    15	```sh
    16	npm install
    17	npm run dev
    18	```
    19	
    20	Open `http://localhost:3000`.
    21	
    22	## Production build
    23	
    24	For Vercel, Netlify, or another Next.js host:
    25	
    26	```sh
    27	npm run build
    28	```
    29	
    30	For a simple static host:
    31	
    32	```sh
    33	npm run build:static
    34	```
    35	
    36	Then upload the generated `out/` folder.
    37	
    38	## Replace the profile photo
    39	
    40	Put your real square profile photo at:
    41	
    42	```txt
    43	public/images/profile-placeholder.png
    44	```
    45	
    46	Keep the filename the same and rebuild.


============================================================
FILE: DEPLOYMENT.md
============================================================

     1	# Deployment Notes
     2	
     3	## Best Option: Vercel
     4	
     5	1. Upload this `portfolio-site` folder to a GitHub repo.
     6	2. Import the repo in Vercel.
     7	3. Use the default Next.js settings.
     8	4. Build command: `npm run build`.
     9	
    10	## Netlify
    11	
    12	1. Upload this folder to a GitHub repo.
    13	2. Connect the repo in Netlify.
    14	3. Build command: `npm run build`.
    15	4. Publish directory: `.next`.
    16	5. Enable Netlify's Next.js runtime if prompted.
    17	
    18	## Simple Static Hosting
    19	
    20	Use this when the host only accepts HTML/CSS/JS files:
    21	
    22	```sh
    23	npm install
    24	npm run build:static
    25	```
    26	
    27	Upload the contents of:
    28	
    29	```txt
    30	out/
    31	```
    32	
    33	This mode does not need a running Node.js server after upload.
    34	
    35	## Contact Button
    36	
    37	The contact button currently uses:
    38	
    39	```txt
    40	mailto:hello@example.com
    41	```
    42	
    43	Replace `hello@example.com` in `app/page.tsx` with your real email address.

FILE: .github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml
============================================================

     1	name: Build static export for Hostinger
     2	
     3	on:
     4	  push:
     5	    branches: [main]
     6	  workflow_dispatch:
     7	
     8	permissions:
     9	  contents: write
    10	
    11	concurrency:
    12	  group: hostinger-static-export
    13	  cancel-in-progress: true
    14	
    15	jobs:
    16	  build-and-publish:
    17	    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    18	    steps:
    19	      - name: Check out source
    20	        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    21	
    22	      - name: Set up Node
    23	        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
    24	        with:
    25	          node-version: 22
    26	          cache: npm
    27	
    28	      - name: Install dependencies
    29	        run: npm ci
    30	
    31	      - name: Build static export
    32	        run: npm run build:static
    33	
    34	      - name: Publish static export branch
    35	        env:
    36	          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
    37	        run: |
    38	          cd out
    39	          git init -b hostinger-deploy
    40	          git config user.name "github-actions[bot]"
    41	          git config user.email "41898282+github-actions[bot]@users.noreply.github.com"
    42	          git add -A
    43	          git commit -m "Deploy static export from ${GITHUB_SHA}"
    44	          git remote add origin "https://x-access-token:${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com/${GITHUB_REPOSITORY}.git"
    45	          git push --force origin hostinger-deploy

FILE: read_project.sh
============================================================

     1	#!/bin/bash
     2	
     3	PROJECT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
     4	OUTPUT_FILE="$PROJECT_DIR/portfolio_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt"
     5	
     6	echo "Generating Portfolio project snapshot..."
     7	echo "Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR"
     8	echo "Snapshot: $OUTPUT_FILE"
     9	echo ""
    10	
    11	write_header() {
    12	  cat > "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
    13	Portfolio Next.js PROJECT SNAPSHOT
    14	Generated: $(date)
    15	Project directory: $PROJECT_DIR
    16	
    17	IMPORTANT INSTRUCTIONS FOR CHATGPT & CODEX:
    18	1. Read this entire snapshot before giving code edits.
    19	2. Preserve all assets, spacing, alignment, fonts, images, layout, animations, and relative positioning unless specifically asked to change them.
    20	3. Do not remove unrelated code.
    21	4. Do not rewrite large sections unless requested.
    22	5. When giving code changes, include exact line/row numbers whenever possible.
    23	6. Print full scripts when requested.
    24	7. Treat this project structure as the source of truth.
    25	8. If you do not understand something, ask for clarification instead of making assumptions.
    26	9. Warn me, or at least caution me, if I ask for something that would break the project or cause it to not work as intended.
    27	10. For this Portfolio repo/website project, always ask me for the commit message before committing or pushing.
    28	
    29	
    30	============================================================
    31	PORTFOLIO PROJECT NOTES
    32	============================================================
    33	
    34	- Main source branch: main
    35	- Deployment source: GitHub Actions builds main into the hostinger-deploy branch
    36	- Hostinger root directory: public_html
    37	- Main stack represented by the website: React / React Native, Next.js, Tailwind CSS, Node.js, Kotlin
    38	- Public website assets live under public/
    39	- Run this script before starting a Portfolio session to refresh the project snapshot.
    40	
    41	
    42	============================================================
    43	SNAPSHOT IGNORE POLICY
    44	============================================================
    45	
    46	This file is rebuilt from the current Portfolio workspace every time ./read_project.sh runs.
    47	Ignored paths are omitted from the project tree and file dumps unless they appear inside an included text file such as .gitignore or read_project.sh.
    48	
    49	The snapshot intentionally ignores generated/cache/runtime/private files:
    50	- .git
    51	- node_modules
    52	- .next
    53	- .next.*
    54	- out
    55	- dist
    56	- build
    57	- .cache
    58	- .turbo
    59	- .npm
    60	- .pnpm-store
    61	- .yarn
    62	- .DS_Store
    63	- next-env.d.ts
    64	- *.tsbuildinfo
    65	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
    66	- *.zip
    67	- .env and .env*.local
    68	
    69	Binary image, PDF, and archive files are inventoried by path, size, and modified time only. Raw binary bytes are not printed.
    70	
    71	
    72	============================================================
    73	PROJECT FILE TREE
    74	============================================================
    75	
    76	EOF
    77	}
    78	
    79	print_file() {
    80	  local file="$1"
    81	
    82	  if [ -f "$file" ]; then
    83	    {
    84	      echo ""
    85	      echo ""
    86	      echo "============================================================"
    87	      echo "FILE: ${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}"
    88	      echo "============================================================"
    89	      echo ""
    90	      nl -ba "$file"
    91	    } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    92	  fi
    93	}
    94	
    95	append_project_tree() {
    96	  # Use Git's own ignore rules so the snapshot cannot drift from .gitignore.
    97	  # Include tracked files plus untracked files that Git does not ignore.
    98	  printf '%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
    99	
   100	  git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" ls-files --cached --others --exclude-standard \
   101	    | sort \
   102	    | while IFS= read -r relative_file; do
   103	      case "$relative_file" in
   104	        .git/*|node_modules/*|.next/*|.next.*/*|out/*|dist/*|build/*|.cache/*|.turbo/*|.npm/*|.pnpm-store/*|.yarn/*|.DS_Store|*/.DS_Store|*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt|*.zip|.env|.env*.local|next-env.d.ts|*.tsbuildinfo)
   105	          continue
   106	          ;;
   107	      esac
   108	
   109	      printf '%s/%s\n' "$PROJECT_DIR" "$relative_file" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   110	    done
   111	}
   112	
   113	print_inventory_header() {
   114	  local title="$1"
   115	
   116	  {
   117	    echo ""
   118	    echo ""
   119	    echo "============================================================"
   120	    echo "$title"
   121	    echo "============================================================"
   122	    echo ""
   123	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   124	}
   125	
   126	print_file_inventory_line() {
   127	  local file="$1"
   128	  local size
   129	  local modified
   130	
   131	  size="$(stat -f "%z" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%s" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   132	  modified="$(stat -f "%Sm" -t "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S" "$file" 2>/dev/null || stat -c "%y" "$file" 2>/dev/null || echo "unknown")"
   133	  printf '%s | %s bytes | modified %s\n' "${file#$PROJECT_DIR/}" "$size" "$modified" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   134	}
   135	
   136	print_dir_inventory() {
   137	  local title="$1"
   138	  local dir="$2"
   139	
   140	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   141	
   142	  if [ ! -d "$dir" ]; then
   143	    echo "Directory not present: ${dir#$PROJECT_DIR/}" >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   144	    return
   145	  fi
   146	
   147	  find "$dir" -type f \
   148	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   149	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   150	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   151	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   152	  done
   153	}
   154	
   155	print_matching_inventory() {
   156	  local title="$1"
   157	  shift
   158	
   159	  print_inventory_header "$title"
   160	
   161	  find "$PROJECT_DIR" "$@" -type f \
   162	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.git/*" \
   163	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/node_modules/*" \
   164	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next/*" \
   165	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/.next.*/*" \
   166	    ! -path "$PROJECT_DIR/out/*" \
   167	    ! -name ".DS_Store" \
   168	    ! -name "*_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt" \
   169	    | sort | while IFS= read -r file; do
   170	    print_file_inventory_line "$file"
   171	  done
   172	}
   173	
   174	append_git_context() {
   175	  {
   176	    echo ""
   177	    echo ""
   178	    echo "============================================================"
   179	    echo "GIT CONTEXT"
   180	    echo "============================================================"
   181	    echo ""
   182	    echo "Current branch:"
   183	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" branch --show-current 2>/dev/null || true
   184	    echo ""
   185	    echo "Status:"
   186	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" status --short --branch 2>/dev/null || true
   187	    echo ""
   188	    echo "Recent commits:"
   189	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" log --oneline --decorate -10 2>/dev/null || true
   190	    echo ""
   191	    echo "Remotes:"
   192	    git -C "$PROJECT_DIR" remote -v 2>/dev/null || true
   193	  } >> "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   194	}
   195	
   196	write_snapshot() {
   197	  write_header
   198	
   199	  append_project_tree
   200	
   201	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   202	
   203	
   204	============================================================
   205	FILE CONTENTS - APP SOURCE
   206	============================================================
   207	
   208	EOF
   209	
   210	  for file in \
   211	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/layout.tsx" \
   212	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/page.tsx" \
   213	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/ProjectCarouselButton.tsx" \
   214	    "$PROJECT_DIR/app/globals.css"
   215	  do
   216	    print_file "$file"
   217	  done
   218	
   219	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   220	
   221	
   222	============================================================
   223	FILE CONTENTS - ROOT / TOOLING / DEPLOYMENT
   224	============================================================
   225	
   226	EOF
   227	
   228	  for file in \
   229	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.gitignore" \
   230	    "$PROJECT_DIR/README.md" \
   231	    "$PROJECT_DIR/DEPLOYMENT.md" \
   232	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package.json" \
   233	    "$PROJECT_DIR/package-lock.json" \
   234	    "$PROJECT_DIR/next.config.ts" \
   235	    "$PROJECT_DIR/postcss.config.mjs" \
   236	    "$PROJECT_DIR/tsconfig.json" \
   237	    "$PROJECT_DIR/.github/workflows/hostinger-static-export.yml" \
   238	    "$PROJECT_DIR/public/favicon.svg" \
   239	    "$PROJECT_DIR/read_project.sh"
   240	  do
   241	    print_file "$file"
   242	  done
   243	
   244	  append_git_context
   245	
   246	  print_dir_inventory "BINARY / IMAGE ASSET INVENTORY - PUBLIC IMAGES" "$PROJECT_DIR/public/images"
   247	  print_matching_inventory "PDF WORKLOG INVENTORY" \( -name "portfolio_worklog_*.pdf" \)
   248	  print_matching_inventory "DEPLOYMENT ARCHIVE INVENTORY" \( -name "*.zip" \)
   249	
   250	  cat >> "$OUTPUT_FILE" <<EOF
   251	
   252	
   253	============================================================
   254	NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
   255	============================================================
   256	
   257	The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
   258	- .git
   259	- node_modules
   260	- .next
   261	- .next.*
   262	- out
   263	- dist
   264	- build
   265	- .cache
   266	- .turbo
   267	- .npm
   268	- .pnpm-store
   269	- .yarn
   270	- .DS_Store
   271	- next-env.d.ts
   272	- *.tsbuildinfo
   273	- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
   274	- *.zip
   275	- .env and .env*.local
   276	
   277	The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
   278	
   279	EOF
   280	}
   281	
   282	write_snapshot
   283	
   284	echo "Done."
   285	echo ""
   286	echo "Snapshot created here:"
   287	echo "$OUTPUT_FILE"
   288	echo ""
   289	echo "Open it with:"
   290	echo "open \"$OUTPUT_FILE\""

============================================================
NOT INCLUDED IN SNAPSHOT
============================================================

The Portfolio snapshot intentionally excludes generated/cache/runtime/private paths:
- .git
- node_modules
- .next
- .next.*
- out
- dist
- build
- .cache
- .turbo
- .npm
- .pnpm-store
- .yarn
- .DS_Store
- next-env.d.ts
- *.tsbuildinfo
- *_PROJECT_SNAPSHOT.txt
- *.zip
- .env and .env*.local

The snapshot lists public images, PDF worklogs, and deployment archives by path, size, and modified time, but does not print raw PNG/JPG/PDF/ZIP bytes.
